| `GET /school/psychometric-records` | Authenticated | Coordinator (read-only, own institution) / Parent/Teacher (own scope) | No `Draft`/`Published` gate, same as career records. |
| `POST`/`PATCH /school/psychometric-team/records[/{id}]`, `GET /school/psychometric-team/records`, `GET /school/psychometric-records`, `…/overview` (`psychometric.assessments`), `…/portfolio` (`psychometric_report`), `…/360-view` (Psychometric tab) — **Addendum, 2026-09-28 (`ENH-027`, `DEC-SCOPE-035`)** | as the rows above | as the rows above (only Psychometric Team writes) | **Additive.** Ten optional result keys: `test_date`, `parent_discussion_on`, `follow_up_on` (`YYYY-MM-DD` only; `""` ⇒ `null`; a datetime, a number or an impossible date is **422**), `strengths`, `interest_areas`, `personality_indicators`, `recommended_careers`, `recommended_stream` (string lists, ≤20 × ≤80, trimmed, case-insensitive de-dup, empty ⇒ `null`), `counsellor_remarks` (≤4000) and `parent_discussion_notes` (≤2000) (line breaks kept; control and bidi-override characters refused). POST: optional. PATCH: an absent key is unchanged, `null` clears. Result keys never change `status` and never notify parents; `report_url` keeps its assigned→completed + notification behaviour. Validation runs after the role/portfolio/tier checks (a 403 always wins) and before any write (a **422** writes nothing, `report_url` included). **422** `"<field> <reason>"`, never echoing the value; the existing messages are unchanged; other keys keep today's handling (ignored — `school_student_id`, `psychometric_team_user_id`, `status`, `assessment_type` in a PATCH change nothing). Every read shape above adds the ten keys (`null` = not recorded); which endpoints return `report_url` is unchanged (`/school/psychometric-records` still does not — Client Question #20). Audit: create metadata adds `fields`, update metadata becomes `{"fields": [...]}` (names only). |
| `GET /school/students/{id}/overview` | Authenticated | Parent (own child(ren)) / Teacher (assigned) / Coordinator, Principal (own institution) | `SCH-007` (added 2026-09-15): one child's full picture — `student` (+ `school_name`, `assigned_teacher_name`), `career_guidance{status,sessions}`, `counselling{status,notes}`, `recommended_careers[]`, `psychometric{status,assessments}`, `results[]` (Published only, `SCH-006-AC02`), `activities{attended[],upcoming[]}`. Same scope loader as `GET /school/students/{id}`; a student outside the caller's scope is 403 even by direct ID. No skills/portfolio/overseas fields — none confirmed (`DEC-SCOPE-015`). **ENH-011 addendum (2026-09-22, `DEC-SCOPE-026`):** additive `skills: {soft_skills, digital_skills}` key, each `{status, enrollments[]}` (status `certified` > `completed` > `in_progress` > `not_started`). |
| *(notification side-effects)* `POST /school/psychometric-team/records`, `PATCH .../records/{id}` (report attached), `POST /school/career-counselor/records`, `POST /school/activities`, `POST /school/academic-team/results/{id}/publish` | — | — | `SCH-007`: each write also creates one `Notification` per linked Parent (school-wide, once per Parent, for `/activities`) and one `NotificationDelivery` (`channel=email`) with the real send outcome; the Parent reads the feed via the existing `GET /workflows/notifications`. Verify and Draft steps never notify. |
| `GET /school/students/{id}/timeline` | Authenticated | Parent (own child(ren)) / Teacher (assigned) / Coordinator, Principal (own institution) | `SCH-008` (added 2026-09-15): a chronologically-sorted `events[]` list (`date`, `category` — profile/career/psychometric/academic/activity, `type`, `title`, `detail`) derived purely from existing `SCH-001`/`004`/`005`/`006` rows — no new tables. Same scope loader as `GET /school/students/{id}/overview`; a student outside the caller's scope is 403 even by direct ID. A Draft/Verified result is never included (`SCH-006-AC02`). **ENH-011 addendum (2026-09-22, `DEC-SCOPE-026`):** adds categories `soft_skills`/`digital_skills` (`skill_enrolled`, `skill_completed`, `skill_certified`). |
| `GET /school/global-education/pipeline` — **Addendum, 2026-09-29 (`ENH-017`, `DEC-SCOPE-036`)** | Authenticated | School Coordinator, Principal — own school only (school derived from the session; no id parameter). Every other role `403`; no linked school `403`; unauthenticated `401`. | Read-only. Query: `grade` (int 8–12, optional; same `grade_key` rule as `ENH-016`), `limit` (1–100, default 25), `offset` (0–`MAX_OFFSET`); invalid values `422` (a wrong role still gets `403` first). Response `GlobalEducationPipelineOut` (snake_case): `grade`, `students_in_scope`, `bridged_students`, `funnel[]` (`key`,`label`,`count`; tracked stages pathway, profile evaluation, shortlisted, offer, visa, admitted — cumulative per student), `not_tracked[]` (`key`,`label`,`note`; applications started/submitted, deposit, scholarship, top 100, alumni), `students{items[],total,limit,offset}` with items `school_student_id`,`full_name`,`student_code`,`grade`,`furthest_stage`,`furthest_stage_label`,`visa_stage_label`,`application_count`. **High-level stage only (`School CRM.md` §19):** no university, course, country, overseas record id, notes, reference, offer letter, document, counselor or agent field is ever selected or returned. An offset past the end returns `items: []` with the real `total`. Writes nothing; one `school_global_education_view` log line (ids and counts only). Full contract: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-enh-017-global-education-pipeline-design.md` §6. |
| `GET /school/reports/school-summary` — **Addendum, 2026-09-29 (`ENH-015`, `DEC-SCOPE-037` provisional)** | Authenticated | School Coordinator, Principal — own school only (school from the session; no id parameter). Every other role `403`; no linked school `403`; unauthenticated `401`. | No query, no body. `200 application/pdf` attachment `school-report.pdf` with `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox`. Content: §30 management figures (total students, career guidance, psychometric completed, counselling, skills programs, global education aspirants — the `ENH-016` indicator sets) and the §29 grade-wise table (same `grade_table` as `GET /school/analytics/grade-performance`). Generated in memory, never stored. Render failure `500` `{"detail": "Could not generate the report; please try again"}`. Writes nothing; one `school_report_generated` log line (ids and counts only). Full contract: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-29-enh-015-reports-downloads-design.md` §6. |
| `GET /school/students/{student_id}/progress-report` — **Addendum, 2026-09-29 (`ENH-015`, `DEC-SCOPE-037` provisional)** | Authenticated | School Parent (linked children only), School Coordinator, Principal (own institution). Any other role `403` **before** path validation. Scope and errors are exactly `GET /school/students/{id}/overview`'s (`_load_readable_student`): unknown id `404`, existing but out-of-scope `403`; malformed id from an allowed role `422`. | `200 application/pdf` attachment `progress-report.pdf`, same headers as above. Content: the SCH-007 overview for that reader (published results only; never `report_url`). One `AuditLog` row (`school.progress_report_download`, entity `school_student`, metadata `{role}`) committed before the response — if it cannot be written no PDF is returned. Render failure `500` as above. Full contract: spec §6. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/academic-years` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | Creates a global academic year (ENH-001). Body: `{label, start_date, end_date}` (all required; dates in YYYY-MM-DD format). Returns created year with `{id, label, start_date, end_date, status}` where `status` is `"draft"`. **409** if an academic year with the same `label` already exists. **422** if dates are invalid or `end_date ≤ start_date`. |
| `PATCH /overseas-admin/academic-years/{year_id}` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | Updates an academic year's status (ENH-001). Body: `{status}` (one of `"draft"`, `"active"`, `"closed"`). Status transitions are forward-only (`draft` → `active` → `closed`); **409** if attempting a backward transition. **422** if status value is unrecognized. Returns updated year with `{id, label, start_date, end_date, status}`. Nothing prevents more than one row from being `status="active"` simultaneously; callers resolve "the" active year by most recent `start_date` (a documented best-effort convention, not a uniqueness guarantee). |
| `GET /overseas-admin/academic-years` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | Lists all academic years (ENH-001, not paginated). Returns array of `{id, label, start_date, end_date, status}` ordered by `start_date` descending. |
| `GET /school/academic-years/active` | Authenticated | Any School-domain role (School Coordinator, Principal, Teacher, Parent, Academic Team, Career Counselor, Psychometric Team, School Partnership Manager, EduSphere School Manager, Overseas Admin, Super Admin) | Returns the currently active academic year (ENH-001), or `null` if none is active. Never returns **404**. Response shape (when active exists): `{id, label, start_date, end_date, status}`. |
| `POST /school/students/promotions` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution only | **ENH-004 / `DEC-SCOPE-020`.** Body `{items: [{student_id, action: "promote"\|"hold_back", grade_or_class?}]}`, 1–500 items; `grade_or_class` (≤60 chars) only with `promote`. Moves each student into the **active** academic year: `promote` adds one to `grade_level` and advances the number inside `grade_or_class` (an unswappable label needs the override); `hold_back` keeps grade and label. Always **200** with `{academic_year, counts, results[{student_id, status: promoted\|held_back\|failed\|skipped, reason, message, grade_level, grade_or_class}]}` (200 even when nothing changed, unlike `bulk-upload`'s 201, because a request can create nothing). Row `reason` codes (stable): `already_in_active_year`, `grade_level_not_set`, `terminal_grade`, `label_unparseable`. Valid rows commit; failed/skipped rows change nothing. **Errors:** 401; 403 if the caller is not a coordinator (checked before body validation) or if any listed student is unknown **or** at another institution (one generic message, nothing written); 409 if there is no active academic year, on a concurrent-promotion conflict, or if row locks cannot be taken within 5 s; 422 (FastAPI list-shaped `detail`) for an empty/oversized/duplicated/malformed list, an unknown action, a label with `hold_back` or containing control characters, or **any unknown body field** (a client-supplied `school_id` or `academic_year_id` is refused, never ignored). A 403 for a student outside the caller's institution is recorded as `school.student_promotion_denied` (`outcome=denied`, counts only). **Retry:** no `Idempotency-Key`; the natural key is (student, target year), enforced by a row lock plus `UNIQUE (school_student_id, to_academic_year_id)`. A repeat reports `skipped: already_in_active_year` instead of replaying `promoted`, so confirm via `results[].grade_level` or `GET …/grade-history`. |
| `GET /school/students/{id}/grade-history` | Authenticated | Coordinator, Principal (own institution) / Teacher (assigned) / Parent (own child(ren)) | **ENH-004.** `{student: {id, full_name}, history: [{id, action: promoted\|held_back, from: {academic_year_id, academic_year_label, grade_level, grade_or_class}, to: {…}, created_at}]}`, newest first (**ENH-025 addendum:** `from`/`to` also carry `section`, and `from.roll_number` — a year move clears the student's roll number, so the ledger keeps the previous one; `to.roll_number` is always `null`; both `null` on rows recorded before ENH-025); `[]` if never promoted. Same scope loader as `GET /school/students/{id}` (403 outside scope, 404 for an unknown ID). Not paginated (at most one row per academic year per student). The performer is stored but not returned. |
| `GET /school/transfer-destinations` | Authenticated | School Coordinator | **ENH-005 / `DEC-SCOPE-022`.** Every other school as `[{id, name}]`, nothing more. Not paginated: bounded by the number of partner schools (§0.1 marks it explicitly). |
| `POST /school/students/{id}/transfer-requests` | Authenticated | School Coordinator, own institution only | **ENH-005.** Body `{to_school_id, reason?}` (`extra="forbid"`; `reason` ≤ 500 chars, no control or bidirectional-override characters). Files an outgoing request; `201` with the request. An unknown student ID and another school's student give the **identical `403`** ("This student is not at your institution", audited). Same or unknown destination `422`; a second pending request for the student `409`; over 50 open requests `409`; the 31st filing attempt in an hour `429` with `Retry-After`. |
| `POST /school/transfer-requests/incoming` | Authenticated | School Coordinator | **ENH-005.** Body `{student_code, reason?}`; the code is normalised to 8 hex characters (`422` otherwise). **Always `202 {"accepted": true}`** for a well-formed code (the same shape as `POST /auth/forgot-password`) whether it names a student at another school, nobody, the caller's own student, or a duplicate: a row is created only for a real student at a different school. Every attempt is audited with a reason token, never the code. Same cap (`409`) and throttle (`429`), both checked before any lookup. |
| `GET /school/transfer-requests?status=pending&limit=25&offset=0` | Authenticated | School Coordinator | **ENH-005.** The requests **this school filed**, newest first, `{items, total, limit, offset}` (`limit` 1-100; `status` in `pending`/`approved`/`rejected`/`cancelled`/`all`, else `422`). One response schema always: a not-yet-approved *incoming* row has `student_id`, `student_name` and `from_school` set to `null` (the gaining coordinator knows only the code they typed). The other school is not told of a request until an admin decides (`DEC-SCOPE-022` D5). |
| `POST /school/transfer-requests/{id}/cancel` | Authenticated | School Coordinator of the filing school | **ENH-005.** Withdraws a pending request. An unknown request and another school's request give the identical `403`; a decided request is `409`. Serialises with a concurrent admin decision. |
| `GET /school/students/{id}/transfer-history` | Authenticated | Coordinator, Principal (own institution) / Teacher (assigned) / Parent (own child(ren)) | **ENH-005.** `{student: {id, full_name}, history: [{id, decided_at, from_school, to_school}]}`, approved transfers only. **No `reason` and no staff IDs**, so a Parent never receives either. Not paginated (bounded per student). After a transfer the losing school can no longer read the student, so this is the gaining school's and the parent's view. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/school-transfer-requests?status=pending&limit=25&offset=0` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | **ENH-005.** The admin's queue, unredacted, with requester, deciding admin and, for pending rows, a preview `{linked_parents, in_flight_results, to_school_has_portfolio_staff, pending_parent_invite}` computed with one grouped query per count over the page. `pending_parent_invite` (added 2026-09-21, additive, default `false`) is a boolean read from the student row already loaded: `true` means approval will clear a parent invite that has not been accepted; the invited address is never returned. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/school-transfer-requests/{id}/approve` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | **ENH-005.** The one action that moves the student, in one transaction with a bounded lock wait: sets `school_id`, clears the assigned teacher and pending parent email, withdraws Draft/Verified results, re-scopes a parent account only when no other child remains at the losing school (links are never deleted), and writes audit rows in the same transaction. Returns the request with its `outcome` counts. Decided request `409`; the student moved since filing `409` (request stays pending); lock wait exceeded `409`. Notifications go out after the commit and can never undo it. |
| `POST /overseas-admin/school-transfer-requests/{id}/reject` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | **ENH-005.** Optional body `{note?}`. Changes only the request; the note is shown to the filing coordinator and is never copied into audit or logs. A notice to the coordinator who filed an *incoming* request carries the Student ID only. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/school-students/{id}/transfer-history` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | **ENH-005.** Every request for one student, all statuses, with staff names (admin-only). Not paginated (bounded per student). |
| `GET /school/students/{id}/portfolio` | Authenticated | Coordinator/Teacher (assigned)/Principal (read-only), own institution; Parent (own child(ren)); `academic_team`/`career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` (own school portfolio) | **ENH-012.** No `response_model` (matches `student_timeline()`'s own computed-aggregate convention). Returns `{student, completion_percentage, can_edit, profile_complete, academic_achievements[], psychometric_report[], career_guidance[], languages[], entries: {<10 sections>: [...]}, personal_statement}`. `completion_percentage` is over 16 sections (profile + 4 auto-populated + 10 self-entry + personal statement). `can_edit` is `true` only for the 3 writer roles (and, for `school_teacher`, only when assigned to this student). Same 403-not-404 scope masking as `GET /school/students/{id}`. |
| `POST /school/students/{id}/portfolio/entries` | Authenticated | `school_coordinator` (own institution) / `school_teacher` (own institution, assigned only) / `academic_team` (own school portfolio) | **ENH-012.** Body `{section, title, description?, organization?, date_from?, date_to?}` (`extra="forbid"`; `section` one of the 10 fixed values, 422 otherwise; `title` 1–200 chars, no control characters; `description` ≤2000 chars, permits `\n`/`\t`, blocks bidi-override characters (`clean_free_text` house precedent, not the single-line rule); `organization` ≤200 chars, no control characters; `date_to` must not precede `date_from`). `201` with the created entry. Writes an `AuditLog` row with section + student id only — never title/description/organization (free text is never logged). |
| `PATCH /school/students/{id}/portfolio/entries/{entry_id}` | Authenticated | Same as POST above, and only the entry's own student | **ENH-012.** Partial update; unknown entry or an entry belonging to a different student both give `404`. Uses `payload.model_fields_set` to distinguish "field explicitly sent" (applied, including an explicit `null`, which clears it) from "field omitted" (left untouched) — an explicit `{"organization": null}` actually clears a previously-set value. Post-merge date-range validation re-checked (`422` if the merged `date_to` precedes the merged `date_from`; the entry is left unchanged on that rejection). |
| `DELETE /school/students/{id}/portfolio/entries/{entry_id}` | Authenticated | Same as POST above, and only the entry's own student | **ENH-012.** `204` on success. Unknown entry, an already-deleted entry, and an entry belonging to a different student all give `404` (no ownership leak). Writes an `AuditLog` row (section + student id, no free text) before commit. |
| `PATCH /school/students/{id}/portfolio/personal-statement` | Authenticated | Same as POST above | **ENH-012.** Body `{personal_statement?}` (≤4000 chars, permits `\n`/`\t`, blocks bidi-override characters, blank/`null` clears it). Race-safe upsert: `begin_nested()` insert, and on the expected unique-constraint `IntegrityError` (a concurrent first `PATCH`), re-queries and updates the same row instead of a `409` — a second "set the statement" is not a duplicate-intent conflict like a transfer filing. Returns `{personal_statement, updated_at}`. A second `PATCH` always updates the same `PortfolioProfile` row, never creates a second one. |
| `POST /school/activities/{activity_id}/feedback` | Authenticated | `school_coordinator` (own institution) | **ENH-018.** Body `{rating, satisfaction, feedback, suggestions?, trainer_name?}` — scores strict integers 1–5; `feedback` required ≤5000, `suggestions` ≤5000, `trainer_name` ≤200 single-line; house `clean_free_text` rule; unknown fields `422`. `201` → `ActivityFeedbackOut {id, activity_id, trainer_name, rating, satisfaction, feedback, suggestions, submitted_by_name, submitted_at}`. `404 "Activity not found"` for a missing activity or another school's (indistinguishable); `422 "Feedback is only collected for Edusphere activities"` when `activity_type` is null; `422 "Feedback opens once the activity has taken place"` when `scheduled_at` is in the future; `409 "Feedback has already been submitted for this activity"` (one per activity, immutable). |
| `GET /school/activity-feedback?status=all\|awaiting\|submitted&limit=25&offset=0` | Authenticated | `school_coordinator`, `school_principal` (own institution, read) | **ENH-018.** Page `{items, total, limit, offset}` of the school's feedback-eligible activities (typed, already held), `scheduled_at` desc; item `{activity_id, title, activity_type, scheduled_at, participation{present, marked}, feedback: ActivityFeedbackOut\|null}`. Participation is computed from `school_activity_attendance`, never stored. |
| `GET /overseas-admin/school-activity-feedback?school_id&limit=25&offset=0` | Authenticated | Overseas Admin / Super Admin | **ENH-018.** Submitted feedback across all schools, newest submission first; item = `ActivityFeedbackOut` + `{school_id, school_name, activity_title, activity_type, scheduled_at, participation}`. Malformed `school_id` `422`; unknown school → empty page. Submitter email is never returned. |
| `GET /school/students/{id}/360-view` | Authenticated | The same 7 readers as `GET .../portfolio` (shared loader `_load_student_for_reader`) | **ENH-013 / `DEC-SCOPE-028`.** Returns `{student, career_goal, can_edit_career_goal, tabs}`; `tabs` always holds the 16 keys in display order, each `{status: has_data|empty|restricted, count, not_tracked[], data}`. Each tab is projected **per role** from what that role can already read through an existing endpoint (spec §6.3) — a tab it cannot read is `restricted` with empty `data`; service roles get name + school only in `student`. Results are Published only. Validated against `Student360Out` but returned as the dict (documented via `responses=`), so timestamps serialize exactly as `/overview` does. Read-only: no commit, no audit row; one `student_360_view` log line (ids + role). Not paginated (single-student aggregate). Errors: 401; 403 with the loader's existing messages; 404 unknown student; 422 malformed id. Fixed query count (tested). |
| `PATCH /school/students/{id}/career-goal` | Authenticated | `career_counselor`, own school portfolio | **ENH-013.** Body `{career_goal}` (required key; `extra="forbid"`; ≤120 chars, single line, no control/bidi characters; blank/`null` clears). One transaction: scope check, `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the student row (the lock transfer approval takes), scope re-check under the lock (a transfer that committed meanwhile → `403`), then the ENH-022 tier check for `individual_counselling` (Silver+) against the student's current school (`DEC-SCOPE-028` D13: the ENH-022 `403` messages and `school.tier_access_denied` audit row; nothing written), write + `AuditLog` (`school.career_goal_update`, `{old, new}`), commit. Returns `{school_student_id, career_goal, updated_at}`. Naturally retry-safe; concurrent writes are last-write-wins, each audited. `403` "Career Counselor role required" for any other role. |

**Addendum, 2026-09-21 (`ENH-005` / `DEC-SCOPE-022`) — conventions for the ten rows above (the ten `/school/students/…/transfer-*`/`/overseas-admin/…` rows preceding the five `ENH-012` portfolio rows).** Errors are FastAPI `{"detail": "..."}` (this section's rows never emitted §0.3's `{error_code, message}` shape, as for `ENH-004`); the `409`/`403`/`429` `detail` strings are stable and listed in the design spec §5.3a. Two lists (`GET /school/transfer-requests`, `GET /overseas-admin/school-transfer-requests`) use `limit`/`offset` with a `{items, total, limit, offset}` envelope, a deliberate deviation from §0.1's cursor convention, which no endpoint implements. No `Idempotency-Key` (§0.2: not a financial action): every write is naturally safe to retry (a duplicate filing, approve, reject or cancel answers `409` with a message saying the state has already changed). The existing parent read scope changes: a `school_parent` is scoped by their `SchoolParentLink`s, not by the school on their account, and keeps the same `403` messages. `GET /portal/overseas/admin/school-transfers` serves the admin section's read table.

**Addendum, 2026-09-23 (`ENH-012`) — conventions for the five portfolio rows above.** Errors are FastAPI `{"detail": "..."}`, same convention as `ENH-004`/`ENH-005`. No `Idempotency-Key` (§0.2: not a financial action) — every write here is naturally safe to retry except the two-step DELETE-confirm UI pattern, which is a frontend-only concern (`PortfolioPanel.tsx`'s own busy/inFlight guard prevents a double-click from sending two DELETEs). No pagination: `entries` is bounded per student per section, and every list here is small by construction. `GET /school/students/{id}/portfolio` is read by `apps/web/components/PortfolioPanel.tsx` (writer UI: add/edit/delete entries, edit personal statement) via `apps/web/lib/portfolio.ts`'s `loadPortfolio()`.

**Addendum, 2026-09-23 (`ENH-018`) — conventions for the three activity-feedback rows above** (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-23-enh-018-school-activity-feedback-design.md`). Errors are FastAPI `{"detail": "..."}`; the `detail` strings are a contract. No `Idempotency-Key` (§0.2: not a financial action): one feedback per activity is the natural key, enforced by the `uq_activity_feedback_activity` unique constraint, so a retried POST gets `409` and concurrent submits store exactly one row. Role checks are dependencies, so a wrong role is `403` before any `404`/`422`; an account not linked to a school is `403`. The feedback row and its `AuditLog` (`school.activity_feedback_submit`, IDs and scores only — free text is never audited or logged) commit in one transaction. "Edusphere management" is the existing cross-school pair (Overseas Admin, Super Admin); `edusphere_school_manager` remains unmodelled. **Additive changes after browser QA (2026-09-23, QA-018-09/10):** `GET /school/activity-feedback` accepts an optional `activity_id` (UUID; the caller's own school's eligible activity, otherwise an empty page; malformed `422`), and each row of the existing `GET /school/activities` gains `feedback_submitted` (boolean). No existing field, status code or behaviour changed.

**Addendum, 2026-09-23 (`ENH-022` / `DEC-SCOPE-027`) — partnership-tier enforcement on existing write routes.** No new endpoint and no request/response shape change; the only contract change is a new `403` on these routes when the acting school's valid, cumulative tier does not include the service (service key in brackets): `POST /school/activities` [its `activity_type`; `campus_visit` = `monthly_campus_visits`; no type = any valid tier] and `POST /school/activities/{id}/attendance` [the stored activity's type]; `POST /school/career-counselor/records` [`individual_counselling`, every record type]; `POST`/`PATCH /school/psychometric-team/records` [`psychometric_test`]; `POST`/`PATCH /school/academic-team/test-prep-records` [`ielts_coaching`/`sat_coaching`; a PATCH uses the stored `test_type`]; `POST`/`PATCH /school/academic-team/language-records` [`foreign_language_classes`]; the eight `/school/career-counselor/skill-*` writes [`soft_skills`, or `web_designing` for `digital_skills`]; the four `/school/students/{id}/portfolio` writes [`digital_portfolio_creation`]; `POST /overseas-admin/school-students/{id}/applications` [`application_support`]; `POST`/`PATCH /workflows/overseas/visa` on a School-bridged application only [`visa_support`]. `detail` strings are stable: `This school has no active partnership tier.` (no or unknown tier) · `This school's partnership expired on {DD Mon YYYY}.` (`tier_valid_until` before today's Asia/Kolkata date; that date itself is valid) · `This school's {Tier} partnership does not include {service label} (requires {MinTier} or higher).` Check order: existing role and scope checks (their `403`/`404` unchanged — the tier is never revealed to an out-of-scope caller) → validation of the field that decides the service (`422`) → tier (`403`) → remaining validation → write. A tier `403` writes nothing except one `school.tier_access_denied` audit row, so a retry is safe. `GET /school/entitlements` and every read are unchanged, except one label (`DEC-SCOPE-027` D13): the `web_designing` service's `label` is now `"Digital skills"` (was `"Web designing"`); its `key` is unchanged.

**Addendum, 2026-09-23 (`ENH-023` / `DEC-SCOPE-030`) — grandfathered downgrades, transition audit, notifications, on top of the `ENH-022` addendum above.** `PATCH /overseas-admin/schools/{school_id}` now loads the school with `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` (instead of `db.get`) so concurrent tier changes queue, and returns the existing `SchoolOut` body plus an additive `tier_change` field, typed `response_model=SchoolUpdateOut` (`SchoolOut` + `tier_change: TierChangeOut | None`): `null` for a profile-only body (no `tier`/`tier_valid_until` in it), else `{direction: "upgrade"|"downgrade"|"unchanged", from_tier, to_tier, gained: [{key,label}], lost: [{key,label}]}` from `SERVICE_LABELS`. It accepts an optional `expected_tier` precondition on the body; when present and different from the locked row's current tier, nothing is written and it returns `409 "This school's tier changed to {Current} since you looked it up. Look it up again before changing the tier."`; omitted, behaviour is unchanged. `""` for `tier` or `expected_tier` is normalised to `null` (removal). The `school.tier_update` audit row's metadata widens from `{tier}` to exactly `{tier, from_tier, to_tier, direction, gained, lost, tier_valid_until, previous_tier_valid_until}` (the `tier` key is kept for existing readers), and its `created_at` is `func.clock_timestamp()` (not the transaction-start `func.now()`), so two concurrent PATCHes order correctly under the row lock. A same-tier or valid-until-only PATCH still writes this row with `direction="unchanged"` and sends no notification. Post-commit, only when `direction != "unchanged"`: the school's active Coordinator(s)/Principal(s) and the acting admin each get one in-app `Notification` (title cut to 180 characters) plus one email attempt via `schools._notify_parent`, every recipient isolated in its own try/commit/rollback so a delivery failure never fails or undoes the tier change; a failure logs `logger.warning("tier_change_notification_failed", ...)` with `{school_id, recipient_id, error_type}` only, no `exc_info` (S1). New `GET /overseas-admin/schools/{school_id}/tier-change-preview?tier=` (Overseas Admin/Super Admin, same role check as the PATCH; `403`/`404`/`422`; `tier` optional, `""`/absent means removal): returns the same `tier_change` shape (`response_model=TierChangeOut`) computed from the school's current tier, read-only, no lock, writes nothing. `PATCH`/`DELETE .../portfolio/entries/{id}` now return `404` for an unknown entry before any tier `403` (an entry lookup precedes the tier check on these two routes only, since the anchor is the entry's own `created_at`).

**The `ENH-022` helper's grandfather rule.** `require_school_entitlement` (and its `school_skills.py`/`workflows.py` counterparts, `_require_module_entitlement`/`_require_bridged_visa_entitlement`) gain one optional keyword-only argument, `grandfathered_since: datetime | None`. Omitted, behaviour is byte-for-byte `ENH-022`'s (`test_enh_022_tier_enforcement.py`/`test_enh_022_tier_rules.py` pass unedited). Passed, a would-be `not_included`/`no_tier` denial (never `expired` — D6) is overturned when the school's `tier_valid_until` has not passed and some `school.tier_update` audit row with `created_at > grandfathered_since` records that service as `lost` (or, for a free-text activity with no `service_key`, records a removal, `to_tier: null` + `direction="downgrade"`); legacy rows with no `lost`/`direction` keys never match. A grandfathered allow adds (does not commit) one `school.tier_grandfathered` `AuditLog` row, metadata exactly `{service_key, reason, tier, grandfathered_since}`, riding on the route's own commit — discarded if the route fails after the check — and writes no denial row; a database error in the lookup propagates (fails closed). `grandfathered_since` is passed by exactly **14** routes, each anchored on a row the route already loaded and scope-checked (the spec's own §6 table says "15"; verified against the merged code, 2026-09-23, and corrected here): 4 in `schools.py` (`POST /school/activities/{id}/attendance`; `PATCH .../psychometric-team/records/{id}`; `PATCH .../academic-team/test-prep-records/{id}`; `PATCH .../academic-team/language-records/{id}`), 6 in `school_skills.py` (`PATCH skill-batches/{id}`; `POST skill-batches/{id}/sessions`; `POST skill-batches/{id}/assessments`; `PUT skill-sessions/{id}/attendance`; `PUT skill-assessments/{id}/scores`; `PATCH skill-enrollments/{id}`), 3 in `portfolio.py` (`PATCH`/`DELETE .../portfolio/entries/{id}`; `PATCH .../portfolio/personal-statement`), 1 in `workflows.py` (`PATCH /workflows/overseas/visa/{id}`). Every create route (activity, career/psychometric/test-prep/language record, skill-batch, skill-batch enrollment, portfolio entry, bridged application, visa case) and `PATCH .../career-goal` never pass it and stay fully gated on the current tier.

**Addendum, 2026-09-28 (`ENH-024` / `DEC-SCOPE-033`) — Skill India certification on the five portfolio rows above; additive only.** Design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-enh-024-skill-india-certification-design.md` §5. No new endpoint. Every portfolio entry in a response (`POST`/`PATCH` bodies, `GET /school/students/{id}/portfolio` `entries.*`, and `GET /school/students/{id}/360-view` `tabs.certificates.data.entries` / `tabs.activities.data.portfolio_entries` / `tabs.skills.data.portfolio_entries`) gains four always-present fields, `null` on every entry that is not a Skill India certification: `certification_type` (`"skill_india"`), `certification_status` (`"enrolled"`/`"in_progress"`/`"certified"`), `certificate_number` (≤100 chars), `issued_on` (date). `POST .../portfolio/entries` accepts the four fields, all optional: `certification_type` only on `section="certification"`; a tagged entry needs a status; `certified` needs `certificate_number` and `issued_on`; a non-null status/number/date without the tag is refused (explicit `null`s are accepted, so a client sending the full shape keeps working) — each a `422` list entry with a plain-language message. `certificate_number` is trimmed, blank → `null`, control characters refused. `PATCH .../portfolio/entries/{entry_id}` accepts `certification_status`, `certificate_number`, `issued_on` (same field rules, `model_fields_set` merge as today) and re-checks the same rules on the merged entry, answering `422` with a string `detail` (the `DATE_RANGE_ERROR` precedent) and writing nothing; `certification_type` is never accepted on `PATCH` (`extra="forbid"` → `422`): the tag is set at creation only. The `PATCH` now loads the entry with `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` (the same `404` for an unknown entry or another student's entry, still before the tier check), so concurrent edits of one entry validate against each other's committed result instead of producing a `500` from the new database CHECKs. Audit metadata adds `certification_type` (+ `certification_status` on create; `old_status`/`new_status` on an update that changes it) for tagged entries only — untagged entries' audit rows are unchanged — and never the certificate number; log lines add `certification_type` only. Authorization, tier gating (`digital_portfolio_creation`, ENH-023 grandfathering on `PATCH`/`DELETE`) and `completion_percentage` are unchanged. No `Idempotency-Key`, no rate limit (spec D14/D15).

**Not specified — genuinely open, not invented:** exact request/response field shapes for the three
service-delivery modules (`DATA_MODEL.md` §6.16–6.18 each flag their own field list as open), the
`academic_team`/`career_counselor`/`psychometric_team` assignment-scope mechanism, and how those three
roles' own accounts are provisioned (`SCH-003`'s invite flow covers only the four school-side roles).
Do not build against an assumed answer to any of these — confirm first.

### ENH-031 — Lookups (read-only, `DEC-SCOPE-039`, 2026-09-29)

`GET /api/v1/lookups/{name}` — query `q` (≤ 100 chars, literal case-insensitive substring), `limit` (1–50, default 20). Response `{"items": [{"id", "label", "detail"}], "truncated": bool}`, ordered by label. 403 `"This role cannot use this lookup"` for other roles; the agent gate messages for pending/suspended/deactivated agents; 422 for bad parameters. One `app.lookups` log line per call (counts only, never the search text), no audit row. Each lookup applies the scope of the write endpoint it feeds, so a picker never offers a value that write would refuse. No write endpoint changes.

| name | Roles | Scope | `label` / `detail` |
|---|---|---|---|
| `overseas-students` | overseas_admin, super_admin, counselor, agent | admin all; counselor students of own applications; agent students linked to own agency | full name / email |
| `overseas-students?purpose=link` | agent | any overseas student not linked to own agency; `q` ≥ 3 else 422; ≤ 10; email matches only in full (case-insensitive), names only from the start of a word; 30 searches/minute per agent → `429` + `Retry-After`; each search writes one `lookup.agent_link_search` audit row without the text | full name / masked email (`a***@domain`) |
| `overseas-applications` (`student_id=` optional) | overseas_student, counselor, university_rep, agent, overseas_admin, super_admin | `_assigned_application` rule (student own; counselor own; university_rep own university; agent own agency; admin all) | student (school student's name for bridged rows) / university · course · status |
| `it-job-applications` | placement_team, hr_team, it_admin, super_admin | all | candidate / job title · company · status |
| `schools` | overseas_admin, super_admin, counselor | all partner schools | name / school code |
| `school-students?school_id=` (required; 404 unknown) | overseas_admin, super_admin, counselor | that one school only | full name / grade · student code |

## 12B. BDM appointments (`bdm-006`) — addendum, 2026-10-03

`DEC-SCOPE-068`; spec §5.3 / §5.8; `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.13; `DATA_MODEL.md` "BDM Appointments". Every write returns `{appointment}` (full detail); the list returns `{items, total, limit, offset}`. No DELETE. Errors: string `detail` for 403 / 404 / 409, FastAPI's list for 422, and `{message, code: "possible_overlap", matches, total}` only for the overlap 409. Not idempotent and no `Idempotency-Key` (R-A5).

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /bdm/appointments` | Authenticated | `bdm` (own), `bdm_manager` (team), `super_admin` (all) | Filters `date_from`, `date_to` (IST, inclusive), `status` (repeatable), `appointment_type`, `organization_id`, `bdm_user_id` (manager / super_admin only; from a `bdm` 422), `q`, `limit` ≤ 100, `offset`. `date_from > date_to` 422; other roles 403 |
| `POST /bdm/appointments` | Authenticated | `bdm` (assignee of the organization) | 201. Rule order: 403 role / profile, 404 organization out of type scope, 403 not assignee, 422 archived, 422 foreign contact, 422 type not for the BDM's type, 422 past `starts_at`, 409 `possible_overlap` unless `confirm_overlap` |
| `GET /bdm/appointments/{id}` | Authenticated | `bdm`, `bdm_manager`, `super_admin` in scope | Detail + `events[]` + `permissions`. 404 out of scope or unknown |
| `PATCH /bdm/appointments/{id}` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | Open statuses only (409 otherwise); unknown / server-owned field 422; foreign contact 422; no-op 200 without audit; changed duration re-runs the overlap check (409) |
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/confirm` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | `scheduled` / `rescheduled` to `confirmed`; 409 otherwise |
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/reschedule` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | Open to `rescheduled`; future `starts_at` and different from the current minute (422); overlap 409; 409 when terminal |
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/cancel` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | Open to `cancelled`; `reason` required (422); 409 when terminal |
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/no-show` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | Open to `no_show`; `reason` required and start passed (422); 409 when terminal |
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/complete` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | Open to `completed`; start passed and `outcome` valid for the BDM's type (422); optional `next_follow_up_on` not before today in IST (422); 409 when terminal |

Any write by a non-owner (manager, super_admin, other BDM) is 403 after the 404 scope check.

**Addendum, 2026-10-05 (`bdm-007`, `DEC-SCOPE-070`):**

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST /bdm/appointments/{id}/complete` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | **Body changed:** the meeting report — `outcome` (owner's type list, 422), `discussion` (required, ≤ 4000), `requirements` / `opportunity` (≤ 2000), `next_action` (≤ 1000), `responsible_person` (≤ 200, one line), `next_follow_up_on` (on or after IST today, 422). One transaction: status, report, follow-up, event, audit. Not open 409; before start 422 |
| `PATCH /bdm/appointments/{id}/report` | Authenticated | `bdm` (owner) | Any subset of the report fields; `outcome` / `discussion` not null; `next_follow_up_on: null` clears (follow-up cancelled; set again reopens the same row). No report 409; after the IST filing day or legacy 409; follow-up done 409; equal values are not changes |
| `GET /bdm/appointments` | Authenticated | as above | New filter `outcome_pending` (true = open and past start). Rows gain `outcome_pending` |
| `GET /bdm/appointments/{id}` | Authenticated | as above | Detail gains `report` (or null), `follow_up` `{id, due_on, status}` (or null), `permissions.can_edit_report` |

**Addendum on `GET /bdm/organizations` and `GET /bdm/organizations/{id}` (bdm-002 contract):** `last_meeting_at` / `next_meeting_at` are now computed (bdm-006, spec §5.6): max `starts_at` of completed, min `starts_at` of open future appointments across all BDMs at that organization. Names, types (`string | null`) and nullability are unchanged.

**Addendum, 2026-10-06 (`bdm-008`, `DEC-SCOPE-075`) — follow-ups and tasks.** Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-008-follow-ups-design.md` §6; migration `0077_bdm_tasks_followups`. Scope: `bdm` own (assignee = caller), `bdm_manager` team, `super_admin` all, any other role 403. Every `{id}` outside scope → 404 "Task not found". Today = the IST date of the database clock.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /bdm/tasks` | Authenticated | `bdm`, `bdm_manager`, `super_admin` | `bucket` = `today` (default) / `overdue` / `upcoming` / `open` / `done` / `cancelled`; `kind` (`follow_up` / `task`); `org_type` (7 types or `none`); `organization_id`; `bdm_user_id` (managers only; a `bdm` → 422); `limit` 1–100 (50), `offset`. Response `{items, total, limit, offset, today, counts: {buckets: {today, overdue, upcoming, done, cancelled}, by_org_type: [{org_type, count}]}}` — bucket counts use every filter but `bucket`, type counts every filter but `org_type` (non-zero only). Order: open by `due_on, created_at`; done by `completed_at desc`; cancelled by `cancelled_at desc` |
| `POST /bdm/tasks` | Authenticated | `bdm` | 201 `BdmTaskOut`. Body (`extra=forbid`): `kind`, `title` (1–200, one line), `due_on` (YYYY-MM-DD, ≥ IST today, else 422), `organization_id?` (scope 404; not assigned 403; archived 422), `notes?` (≤ 2000). Server sets `source=manual`, assignee, `open`. 409 after 200 creates in an IST day |
| `PATCH /bdm/tasks/{id}` | Authenticated | `bdm` (assignee) | `title` / `notes` (null clears) / `due_on` (a changed one ≥ IST today). Unchanged values are not changes. 409 when not open, or not manual ("Change this follow-up from its meeting report") |
| `POST /bdm/tasks/{id}/complete` | Authenticated | `bdm` (assignee) | No body. Any open item (outcome follow-ups included). 409 when already done / cancelled |
| `POST /bdm/tasks/{id}/cancel` | Authenticated | `bdm` (assignee) | `{reason}` (≤ 500, required). Manual items only (409 otherwise) |

Write order: scope 404 → lock (an outcome follow-up's appointment first) → assignee 403 ("Only the assigned BDM can change this task", logged) → state 409 → source 409 → 422. `BdmTaskOut`: `id, kind, title, notes, due_on, status, source, overdue, organization {id, code, name, org_type, archived} or null, appointment {id, code} or null, assignee {id, full_name, active}, completed_at, cancelled_at, cancel_reason, created_at, updated_at, permissions {can_edit, can_complete, can_cancel}`. **Changed:** `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/archive` also cancels the organization's open items (reason "Organization archived"); its audit metadata gains `tasks_cancelled` when > 0; the response is unchanged. Unchanged: the appointment's `follow_up {id, due_on, status}`.

---

**Addendum, 2026-10-06 (`bdm-025`, `DEC-SCOPE-082`): BDM deactivation and portfolio handover.**

Sources:
- design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-025-deactivation-handover-design.md` §5;
- migration `0082_bdm_assignment_history`.

Who may call these routes: `ensure_admin`, then the creator types (`super_admin` any; `it_admin` College; `overseas_admin`
Agent / School) → otherwise 403. A non-BDM or unknown id → 404 "BDM not found". The request bodies forbid extra fields.

| Method + path | Body | Success | Refusals |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /admin/bdms/{id}/portfolio` | — | `{organizations, appointments, tasks, trips}`: open work + not-started trips | 403 / 404 |
| `POST /admin/bdms/{id}/deactivate` | `{mode: "reassign" \| "leave", reassign_to: uuid \| null}` | `{id, active: false, mode, moved: {organizations, appointments, tasks}, trips_cancelled}` | 422 no / mismatched choice ("Choose who takes over this BDM's open work"); 422 "Choose an active BDM of the same module"; 409 "This BDM is already inactive" |
| `POST /admin/bdms/{id}/handover` | `{reassign_to: uuid}` | `{id, moved}` | 409 "Deactivate this BDM first"; 409 "No open work to hand over"; 422 invalid target |
| `POST /admin/bdm-managers/{id}/deactivate` | `{reassign_to: uuid \| null}` (required while BDMs report to them) | `{id, active: false, moved_bdms}` | 403 not super_admin; 404 not a manager; 409 already inactive; 422 "Choose another active BDM manager" |

Existing routes:
- `PATCH /admin/users/{id}` with `active: false`:
  - on a BDM → **422** "Deactivate a BDM from the BDMs page, choosing who takes over their open work";
  - on a manager with BDMs → **422** "This manager has N BDMs. Move them to another manager first (BDMs page → BDM managers)";
  - **422, not 409:** the Users page reads a 409 as the trainer "confirm cascade" prompt.
- `GET /admin/bdm-managers` rows add `bdm_count` (additive).
- `POST /bdm/organizations/{id}/assign` also records history.

The routes write in one transaction:
- moves with history rows;
- trip cancels, each with a `bdm.trip_cancel` audit row (`reason: "BDM deactivated"`);
- `active = false` and revoked welcome links;
- an audit row: `bdm.deactivate`, `bdm.portfolio_handover` or `bdm_manager.deactivate`;
- an in-app + email notification to the new owner or manager.

**Addendum, 2026-10-06 (`bdm-013`, `DEC-SCOPE-079`) — BDM calendar.** Design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-bdm-013-calendar-design.md` §5. Read-only; no migration, no audit, no log line.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /bdm/calendar` | Authenticated | `bdm` (own), `bdm_manager` (a BDM reporting to them), `super_admin` (any BDM) | Query `date_from`, `date_to` (required, inclusive IST dates; `date_from > date_to` → `422` "date_from must be on or before date_to"; more than 31 days → `422` "The calendar shows at most 31 days"), `bdm_user_id` (a `bdm` sending it → `422` "bdm_user_id is only for managers"; a manager / super_admin omitting it → `422` "Choose a BDM"; outside the team or not a BDM → `404` "BDM not found"). Other roles `403` "BDM role required"; a BDM without a profile `403`. Response `{bdm: {id, full_name, active}, date_from, date_to, today, truncated, appointments: [{id, code, day, starts_at, duration_minutes, appointment_type, status, seminar, organization: {id, code, name, archived}}], trips: [{id, code, travel_date, return_date, from_place, to_place, mode, approval_status, travel_status}], tasks: [{id, kind, title, due_on, status, overdue, organization \| null}]}`. Excludes cancelled appointments, cancelled or rejected trips, cancelled tasks; a trip is included when it overlaps the range. `day` = the IST date of `starts_at`; `seminar` = type `seminar_workshop`, `seminar`, `workshop` or `student_seminar`. At most 500 rows per list (`truncated: true` when cut) |
| `GET /bdm/my-day` | Authenticated | `bdm` (own) | No parameters (own scope from the session). Other roles `403` "BDM role required"; a BDM without a profile `403`. Response `{today, bdm_type, appointments: {count, truncated, items: [{id, code, starts_at, duration_minutes, appointment_type, status, organization: {id, code, name, org_type, archived}}]}, trips: {total, items: [{id, code, travel_date, return_date, from_place, to_place, approval_status, travel_status, appointment_count}]}, follow_ups: {total, groups: [{key, count}]}, tiles: [{key, label, tracked, value, note}]}`. `bdm-014`, `DEC-SCOPE-097`: today = IST; appointments exclude cancelled (at most 50 listed, `count` exact); trips start after today, not cancelled / rejected (next 5, `total` exact), `appointment_count` = non-cancelled linked appointments; follow-ups = open `follow_up` tasks due ≤ today, grouped by organization type, `none` (no organization) or `mou` (source `mou`); `tiles` = the BDM type's eight Appendix B.2 tiles in order, `value: null` with a `note` when not tracked (T-A6, T-K7). Read-only; a fixed number of statements |

## 12C. Telecaller roles (`tel-001`) — addendum, 2026-10-05

`DEC-SCOPE-073`; design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-tel-001-telecaller-roles-design.md` §5; `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.14; migration `0075_telecaller_profiles`. Lists are `{items, total, limit, offset}` (`limit` default 50, max 100; `offset` ≥ 0; ordered by `full_name, id`). No `/telecaller` route takes a user id; scope comes from the session. GETs are read-only.

| Method/Path | Auth | Roles | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /telecaller/me` | Authenticated | `telecaller` | `{id, full_name, email, phone, active, division, telecaller_profile: {team, employee_id, reporting_manager: {id, full_name, active}}}`. `403` "Telecaller role required" for other roles; `403` "Telecaller profile not set up — contact your administrator" with no profile row |
| `PATCH /telecaller/profile` | Authenticated | `telecaller` | Body `{phone}` only (TL3): trimmed; `""` or `null` clears; max 40; digits, spaces and `+ - ( )`; no control characters. Returns the `GET /telecaller/me` shape. The `phone` key is required (missing → `422`). Any other key → `422` "Unknown field: …"; a bad phone → `422` "Phone may contain only digits, spaces and + - ( )"; other roles `403`. Audit `telecaller.profile_update` with `{"fields": ["phone"]}` (no values) |
| `GET /telecaller/manager/team` | Authenticated | `telecaller_manager` (direct reports), `super_admin` (all) | `{items: [{id, full_name, email, phone, active, team, employee_id}], total, limit, offset}`; inactive telecallers included; optional `q` narrows (name, email, Employee ID). `403` "Telecaller manager role required" otherwise, including a `telecaller` |
| `GET /admin/telecallers` | Authenticated | `super_admin` (both teams), `it_admin` (IT), `overseas_admin` (Overseas) | Same page shape; items add `reporting_manager {id, full_name, active}` and `manager_active`. Filters `team` (`it\|overseas`; any other value `422`; a team outside the caller's → `403` "Your role cannot manage … telecallers"), `active` (bool), `q` (name, email or Employee ID; ANDed with the team scope, never widens it). Other roles `403` "Admin role required" |
| `GET /admin/telecaller-managers` | Authenticated | the three admin roles | `{items: [{id, full_name, email}], total, limit, offset}`: **active** `telecaller_manager` users only; `q` searches name or email (server-search picker; email tells same-name managers apart) |

**`POST /admin/users` — `telecaller` / `telecaller_manager` branch (TL4).** Every other role takes the existing path unchanged. For `role: "telecaller"` the body carries `telecaller_profile: {team: "it"|"overseas", employee_id, reporting_manager_user_id}` (`extra="forbid"`; `employee_id` trimmed, 1–40 characters). Rule order: `422` "Telecaller profile is required" (missing / not an object) → `422` field errors naming the field (e.g. "Employee ID is required") → `403` "Your role cannot manage {IT|Overseas} telecallers" (`it_admin` + `team: "overseas"`) → `422` "Division must match the telecaller's team" (a `division` that differs from `team`; absent defaults to `team`) → the existing steps (password `422`, duplicate email `409`) → `422` "Reporting manager must be an active telecaller manager" → `409` "Employee ID already exists" (case-insensitive unique index). `telecaller_profile` on any other role → `422` "Only a telecaller has a telecaller profile". `role: "telecaller_manager"` (division `global`, no profile): a non-`super_admin` actor → `403` "Only a Super Admin can create telecaller managers". One transaction (user, profile, welcome token, audit); the welcome email is sent after commit. The response keeps every existing key and adds `telecaller_profile` (`{team, employee_id, reporting_manager}` for a telecaller, `null` for every other role), alongside `bdm_profile`. Audit `user.create` metadata gains `telecaller_profile`.

**`PATCH /admin/users/{id}` — `telecaller_profile` branch.** The existing `404` and cross-division `403` are unchanged. For a telecaller target the profile row is locked and `403` "Your role cannot manage … telecallers" applies to a team the actor does not manage. `telecaller_profile` is a partial object (omitted = unchanged): a different `team` → `422` "Team cannot be changed here" (TL7); `team`, `employee_id` or `reporting_manager_user_id` sent as explicit `null` → `422`; a new reporting manager must be an active `telecaller_manager` (`422`); a duplicate Employee ID → `409`; `telecaller_profile` for a non-telecaller → `422` "Only a telecaller has a telecaller profile". Audit `user.update` gains `telecaller_profile_before` / `telecaller_profile_after`. Response `{"ok": true}`. Deactivating a manager uses the existing `active` path; their telecallers then show `manager_active: false` (reassignment is tel-025).

**`PATCH /auth/me` — telecaller lock (TL8).** For role `telecaller` only: a `full_name` that differs from the stored one (after trimming), or a `profile` that differs from the stored one, → `403` "Telecallers can change only their phone number — contact your administrator" (nothing is written). An unchanged `full_name` is accepted, so the generic `/account/profile` form still saves a phone. `phone` is validated with the `PATCH /telecaller/profile` rule (trimmed; `""` / `null` clears; bad characters → `422` "Phone may contain only digits, spaces and + - ( )"). Every other role, including `bdm`, is unchanged.

**`POST /auth/reset-password` — `login_portal` addendum.** The response shape is unchanged: `login_portal` is now `"admin"` for `telecaller_manager` as well as `bdm_manager` (one constant, `ADMIN_PORTAL_ROLES`), and `null` for every other role. A telecaller manager's welcome / reset link opens `/admin/reset-password`; a telecaller's opens its division's page.

---

## 12D. Telecaller catalogue (`tel-002`) — addendum, 2026-10-06

`DEC-SCOPE-074`; design spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-tel-002-catalogue-design.md` §4; `RBAC_MATRIX.md` §2.15; migration
`0076_tel_catalogue`. Lists are `{items, total, limit, offset}` (`limit` default 50, max 100). Write bodies reject unknown keys; every `422`
is one sentence naming the field (e.g. "Name is required", "Source: Input should be …"). Readers = `telecaller`, `telecaller_manager`,
`super_admin`, `it_admin`, `overseas_admin`, `counselor`; writers = `telecaller_manager`, `super_admin`. Other roles → `403`; signed out → `401`.

| Method/Path | Roles | Notes / status codes |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /telecaller/products` | readers | Filters `group` (`it\|overseas\|other`, else `422`), `active`, `q` (name). Non-managers always get active rows only (an `active` param cannot widen it). Order: IT, Overseas, Other, then `sort_order`, then name. Item `{id, group, name, team, program: {id, title} \| null, active, sort_order}` |
| `POST /telecaller/products` | writers | `201`. Body `{group, name, team?, program_id?, sort_order?}`. `team` omitted → the group's team (IT/Overseas) or none (Other); an IT/Overseas `team` ≠ group → `422` "IT products always route to the IT team" / "Overseas products …". `program_id` on a non-IT product → `422` "Only IT products can link to a course"; missing/inactive program → `422` "Choose an active course". `sort_order` omitted → after the group's last product. Duplicate name in the group (case-insensitive) → `409` |
| `PATCH /telecaller/products/{id}` | writers | Body any of `{name, team, program_id, active, sort_order}`; omitted = unchanged; `null` clears `team` (Other only) or `program_id`, else `422`. A different `group` → `422` "Group cannot be changed". Unknown id → `404`. Renaming keeps the id (lead links survive) |
| `GET /telecaller/campaigns` | readers | Filters `product_id`, `source` (one of the 13 keys, else `422`), `active`, `q` (name). Non-managers: active only. Order: active first, newest `start_date`, name. Item `{id, name, source, product: {id, name, group, active}, start_date, end_date, active}` |
| `POST /telecaller/campaigns` | writers | `201`. Body `{name, source, product_id, start_date, end_date?}`. `source` ∈ `instagram, facebook, google, website, whatsapp, walk_in, college, school, agent, referral, exhibition_event, bdm, other`. Missing/inactive product → `422` "Choose an active product". `end_date < start_date` → `422` "End date cannot be before the start date". Duplicate name (case-insensitive, global) → `409` |
| `PATCH /telecaller/campaigns/{id}` | writers | Body any of `{name, source, product_id, start_date, end_date, active}`; `null` clears only `end_date`. The date rule is checked on the merged row. Moving to another product needs an active one; keeping a since-deactivated product is allowed (P4). Unknown id → `404` |

Every write adds an `AuditLog` row (`telecaller.product_create|product_update|campaign_create|campaign_update`, entity `tel_product` /
`tel_campaign`, `metadata_json.fields` = the changed field names only).
